
FUNCTION Main_copy GLOBAL
    # # ── Phase 0: Initialize ──────────────────────────────────────────
    **REGION Initialise Values
    DateTime.GetCurrentDateTime.Local DateTimeFormat: DateTime.DateTimeFormat.DateAndTime CurrentDateTime=> dt_CurrentDateTime
    Text.ConvertDateTimeToText.FromDateTime DateTime: dt_CurrentDateTime StandardFormat: Text.WellKnownDateTimeFormat.SortableDateTime Result=> txt_ProcessStartTime
    SET txt_ExceptionType TO $'''%''%'''
    SET txt_ExceptionMessage TO $'''%''%'''
    SET txt_PreviousExceptionMessage TO $'''%''%'''
    SET flg_SendExceptionEmail TO False
    SET flg_Screenshot TO True
    SET num_ConsecutiveExcCount TO 0
    SET txt_FailedItemsLog TO $'''%''%'''
    SET num_ItemsException TO 0
    SET num_ItemsCompleted TO 0
    SET obj_Config TO {{ }}
    Variables.ConvertJsonToCustomObject Json: In_txt_Config CustomObject=> obj_Config
    IF IsEmpty(obj_Config) THEN
        SET flg_Screenshot TO False
        SET txt_ExceptionMessage TO $'''No data present in config object input'''
        FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''System Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ExceptionMessage
    END
    # # Load all Config data
    CALL 'Load Config Data'
    # # Close all applications
    CALL 'Close Application' In_txt_AppProcessName: txt_AppProcessName + ',' + txt_ExcelProcessName
    **ENDREGION
    BLOCK 'Get unprocessed items'
ON BLOCK ERROR 'Business Exception' IsUserDefinedErrorCode: True
    SET flg_Screenshot TO False
    CALL 'Get Error'
    SET txt_ExceptionType TO $'''Business Exception'''
    GOTO 'Business Error'
ON BLOCK ERROR
    CALL 'Get Error'
    GOTO 'System Error'
END
        @@'InputSummaryValue:WORKQUEUE': '%WorkQueueId%'
WorkQueues.GetWorkQueueItems WorkQueue: txt_WorkQueueId FilterRows: $'''<attribute name=\"statecode\" />
<filter type=\"and\">
    <condition attribute=\"statecode\" operator=\"eq\" value=\"0\" />
</filter>''' RowsToReturn: 5000 WorkQueueItems=> lst_WorkQueueItems
        IF lst_WorkQueueItems.Count = 0 THEN
            SET txt_ExceptionMessage TO 'No work queue items found in: ' + txt_QueueName
            FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''Business Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ExceptionMessage
        END
    END
    BLOCK 'Launch block'
ON BLOCK ERROR
    SET flg_Screenshot TO True
    CALL 'Get Error'
    GOTO 'System Error'
END
        SET flg_IsAppRunning TO False
        SET flg_IsLoggedIn TO False
        CALL 'Launch Application'
    END
    BLOCK 'Main Work block'
ON BLOCK ERROR all
    SET flg_Screenshot TO False
    CALL 'Get Error'
    GOTO 'System Error'
END
        CALL 'Process Work Queue Items'
        GOTO 'End'
    END
    LABEL 'System Error'
    CALL 'Close Application' In_txt_AppProcessName: txt_AppProcessName + ',' + txt_ExcelProcessName
    CALL 'Send System Exception Mail' In_txt_To_Email: obj_Config['Mail_SystemExceptionTo'] In_txt_Cc_Email: obj_Config['Mail_SystemExceptionCc'] In_txt_Subject: $'''%txt_ProcessName% - Consecutive error encountered''' In_txt_ErrorMessage: txt_ExceptionMessage In_lst_Attachments: []
    FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''System Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ExceptionMessage
    LABEL 'Business Error'
    GOTO 'End'
    LABEL 'End'
    CALL 'Close Application' In_txt_AppProcessName: txt_AppProcessName + ',' + txt_ExcelProcessName
    SET out_num_CompletedCount TO num_ItemsCompleted
    SET out_num_ExceptionCount TO num_ItemsException
    SET out_txt_ExceptionDetail TO txt_FailedItemsLog
END FUNCTION
==================================>

FUNCTION 'Load Config Data' GLOBAL
    BLOCK 'Assign values from config'
ON BLOCK ERROR all
    SET flg_Screenshot TO False
    SET flg_ConfigError TO True
    CALL 'Get Error'
    THROW ERROR
END
        # ####################### Get Computer name and User name #######################
        **REGION System Variables
        System.GetEnvironmentVariable.GetEnvironmentVariable Name: $'''USERNAME''' Value=> txt_UserName
        SET txt_ProcessName TO obj_Config['Ctrl_ProcessName']
        System.GetEnvironmentVariable.GetEnvironmentVariable Name: $'''COMPUTERNAME''' Value=> txt_ResourceName
        Text.Trim Text: txt_UserName TrimOption: Text.TrimOption.Both TrimmedText=> txt_UserName
        Text.Trim Text: txt_ProcessName TrimOption: Text.TrimOption.Both TrimmedText=> txt_ProcessName
        Text.Trim Text: txt_ResourceName TrimOption: Text.TrimOption.Both TrimmedText=> txt_ResourceName
        **ENDREGION
        # ####################### Get Limits and Wait times #######################
        **REGION Time Variables
        Text.ToNumber Text: obj_Config['Delay_DownArrowSec'] Number=> num_Delay_DownArrowSec
        Text.ToNumber Text: obj_Config['Delay_XS'] Number=> num_Delay_XS
        Text.ToNumber Text: obj_Config['Delay_S'] Number=> num_Delay_S
        Text.ToNumber Text: obj_Config['Delay_M'] Number=> num_Delay_M
        Text.ToNumber Text: obj_Config['Delay_L'] Number=> num_Delay_L
        Text.ToNumber Text: obj_Config['Delay_XL'] Number=> num_Delay_XL
        Text.ToNumber Text: obj_Config['App_WaitTime'] Number=> num_App_WaitTime
        Text.ToNumber Text: obj_Config['Ctrl_MaxRetryLimit'] Number=> num_MaxRetryLimit
        Text.ToNumber Text: obj_Config['Ctrl_ConsecutiveExceptionLimit'] Number=> num_ConsecutiveExcLimit
        **ENDREGION
        # ####################### Set queue details #######################
        **REGION Queue Variables
        SET txt_QueueName TO obj_Config['Ctrl_QueueName']
        SET txt_WorkQueueId TO obj_Config['Ctrl_WorkQueueId']
        SET txt_ProcessName TO obj_Config['Ctrl_ProcessName']
        **ENDREGION
        # ####################### Set App details #######################
        **REGION Application Variables
        Text.Replace.ReplaceText Text: obj_Config['App_CommandLineParams'] TextToFind: $'''*''' IgnoreCase: False ReplaceWith: $'''%''%''' ActivateEscapeSequences: False ComparisonType: Text.TextComparisonType.CultureSensitive Result=> txt_AppProcessName
        Text.Replace.ReplaceText Text: obj_Config['Excel_CommandLineParams'] TextToFind: $'''*''' IgnoreCase: False ReplaceWith: $'''%''%''' ActivateEscapeSequences: False ComparisonType: Text.TextComparisonType.CultureSensitive Result=> txt_ExcelProcessName
        SET txt_App_SampleManagerExePath TO obj_Config['App_SampleManagerExePath']
        SET txt_App_SampleManagerWindowTitle TO obj_Config['App_SampleManagerWindowTitle']
        **ENDREGION
        # ####################### Set mail folder paths #######################
        **REGION Mail folder variables
        SET txt_MailboxName TO obj_Config['Mail_MailboxName']
        SET txt_MailProcessFolder TO obj_Config['Mail_OutlookFolder']
        SET txt_MailCompletedFolder TO txt_MailProcessFolder + '/' + obj_Config['Mail_EmailCompleted']
        SET txt_MailExceptionFolder TO txt_MailProcessFolder + '/' + obj_Config['Mail_EmailException']
        Text.Replace.ReplaceText Text: txt_MailCompletedFolder TextToFind: $'''\\''' IgnoreCase: False ReplaceWith: $'''/''' ActivateEscapeSequences: False ComparisonType: Text.TextComparisonType.CultureSensitive Result=> txt_MailCompletedFolder
        Text.Replace.ReplaceText Text: txt_MailExceptionFolder TextToFind: $'''\\''' IgnoreCase: False ReplaceWith: $'''/''' ActivateEscapeSequences: False ComparisonType: Text.TextComparisonType.CultureSensitive Result=> txt_MailExceptionFolder
        **ENDREGION
        # ####################### Set local folder paths #######################
        **REGION Local Filepath Variables
        Text.Replace.ReplaceText Text: obj_Config['Path_RootFolder'] TextToFind: $'''<<username>>''' IgnoreCase: False ReplaceWith: txt_UserName ActivateEscapeSequences: False ComparisonType: Text.TextComparisonType.CultureSensitive Result=> txt_RootPath
        IF (Folder.IfFolderExists.DoesNotExist Path: txt_RootPath) THEN
            SET txt_ExceptionMessage TO 'Folder not found: ' + txt_RootPath
            FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''System Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ExceptionMessage
        END
        Text.Replace.ReplaceText Text: obj_Config['Path_ProjectFolder'] TextToFind: $'''<<processname>>''' IgnoreCase: False ReplaceWith: txt_ProcessName ActivateEscapeSequences: False ComparisonType: Text.TextComparisonType.CultureSensitive Result=> txt_ProjectFolder
        SET txt_EmailPath TO $'''%txt_RootPath%%txt_ProjectFolder%%obj_Config['Path_EmailDownload']%'''
        IF (Folder.IfFolderExists.DoesNotExist Path: txt_EmailPath) THEN
            SET txt_ExceptionMessage TO 'Folder not found: ' + txt_EmailPath
            FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''System Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ExceptionMessage
        END
        SET txt_ScreenshotPath TO $'''%txt_RootPath%%txt_ProjectFolder%%obj_Config['Path_ScreenshotFolder']%'''
        IF (Folder.IfFolderExists.DoesNotExist Path: txt_ScreenshotPath) THEN
            Text.CropText.CropTextAfterFlag Text: txt_ProjectFolder FromFlag: $'''\\''' IgnoreCase: False CroppedText=> txt_NewFolder
            Folder.Create FolderPath: txt_RootPath FolderName: $'''%txt_NewFolder%%obj_Config['Path_ScreenshotFolder']%''' Folder=> txt_NewFolder
        END
        SET txt_SummaryReportPath TO $'''%txt_RootPath%%txt_ProjectFolder%%obj_Config['Path_SummaryReport']%'''
        IF (Folder.IfFolderExists.DoesNotExist Path: txt_SummaryReportPath) THEN
            SET txt_ExceptionMessage TO 'Folder not found: ' + txt_SummaryReportPath
            FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''System Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ExceptionMessage
        END
        SET txt_SummaryFileName TO obj_Config['Path_SummaryFileName']
        SET txt_SummaryFilePath TO $'''%txt_SummaryReportPath%\\%txt_SummaryFileName%'''
        Text.Replace.ReplaceText Text: obj_Config['Path_SharePointFilePath'] TextToFind: $'''<<processname>>''' IgnoreCase: False ReplaceWith: txt_ProcessName ActivateEscapeSequences: False ComparisonType: Text.TextComparisonType.CultureSensitive Result=> txt_SharePointFilePath
        Text.Replace.ReplaceText Text: txt_SharePointFilePath TextToFind: $'''<<filename>>''' IgnoreCase: False ReplaceWith: txt_SummaryFileName ActivateEscapeSequences: False ComparisonType: Text.TextComparisonType.CultureSensitive Result=> txt_SharePointFilePath
        IF (File.IfFile.DoesNotExist File: txt_SummaryFilePath) THEN
            # ####################### Download Template file #######################
            SET txt_SharePointSiteUrl TO obj_Config['Path_SharePointSiteUrl']
            @@dataset: '%txt_SharePointSiteUrl%'
@@path: '%txt_SharePointFilePath%'
@@connectionDisplayName: 'SharePoint_PID_171'
External.InvokeCloudConnector Connection: 'a511de2d-1427-40dd-8725-09773756a937' ConnectorId: '/providers/Microsoft.PowerApps/apis/shared_sharepointonline' OperationId: 'GetFileContentByPath' timeout: num_Delay_M @dataset: txt_SharePointSiteUrl @path: txt_SharePointFilePath @inferContentType: True @GetFileContentByPathResponse=> txt_FileContentResponse
            @@dataset: '%txt_SharePointSiteUrl%'
@@id: '%txt_SharePointFilePath%'
@@connectionDisplayName: 'SharePoint_PID_171'
DISABLE External.InvokeCloudConnector Connection: 'a511de2d-1427-40dd-8725-09773756a937' ConnectorId: '/providers/Microsoft.PowerApps/apis/shared_sharepointonline' OperationId: 'GetFileContent' timeout: num_Delay_M @dataset: txt_SharePointSiteUrl @id: txt_SharePointFilePath @inferContentType: True @GetFileContentResponse=> txt_FileContentResponse
            File.ConvertFromBinary BinaryData: txt_FileContentResponse File: txt_SummaryFilePath IfFileExists: File.IfExists.Overwrite
        END
        **ENDREGION
        # ####################### Patterns and keys #######################
        **REGION Secret & Key Variables
        SET txt_Excel_PatternsCsv TO obj_Config['Excel_PatternsCsv']
        SET txt_JpgPatternsCsv TO obj_Config['Excel_JpgPatternsCsv']
        SET txt_EncDecSecret_Key TO obj_Config['Sec_EncDecSecret']
        @@secretName: '%obj_Config[\'Sec_AppSecret\']%'
@@connectionDisplayName: 'Azure Key Vault Shell_PP_PID_US_171_US_PreludeLIMS_V12-6e0e5'
External.InvokeCloudConnector Connection: '0107d20b-9bc5-4f9b-9876-cb0487d93b1c' ConnectorId: '/providers/Microsoft.PowerApps/apis/shared_keyvault' OperationId: 'GetSecret' @secretName: obj_Config['Sec_AppSecret'] @GetSecretResponse=> obj_SecretResponse
        @@secretName: '%obj_Config[\'Sec_DBSecret\']%'
@@connectionDisplayName: 'Azure Key Vault Shell_PP_PID_US_171_US_PreludeLIMS_V12-6e0e5'
External.InvokeCloudConnector Connection: '0107d20b-9bc5-4f9b-9876-cb0487d93b1c' ConnectorId: '/providers/Microsoft.PowerApps/apis/shared_keyvault' OperationId: 'GetSecret' @secretName: obj_Config['Sec_DBSecret'] @GetSecretResponse=> obj_DBSecret
        SET txt_DbPassword TO obj_DBSecret.value
        SET txt_AppPassword TO obj_SecretResponse.value
        SET txt_AppUsername TO obj_Config['Sec_AppUsername']
        **ENDREGION
    END
END FUNCTION

FUNCTION 'Get Error' GLOBAL
    # ############### Capture Error Details ###############
    ERROR => LastError
    SET txt_ExceptionType TO $'''System Exception'''
    SET txt_ExceptionMessage TO $'''Error message :%LastError.Message% |
Flow name : %LastError.SubflowName% |
Action name :%LastError.ActionName%'''
    # ############### Capture Screenshot ###############
    SET txt_LastScreenshotPath TO $'''%''%'''
    IF flg_Screenshot = True THEN
        DateTime.GetCurrentDateTime.Local DateTimeFormat: DateTime.DateTimeFormat.DateAndTime CurrentDateTime=> dt_CurrentDateTimeVal
        Text.ConvertDateTimeToText.FromCustomDateTime DateTime: dt_CurrentDateTimeVal CustomFormat: $'''MMddyy_hhmmss''' Result=> txt_ScreenShotTime
        SET txt_LastScreenshotPath TO $'''%txt_ScreenshotPath%\\%txt_ScreenShotTime%.png'''
        Workstation.TakeScreenshot.TakeScreenshotAndSaveToFile File: txt_LastScreenshotPath ImageFormat: System.ImageFormat.Png
        ON ERROR

        END
        SET flg_Screenshot TO False
    END
END FUNCTION
==================================>

FUNCTION 'Fetch Data from Excel file' in_txt_InputFilePath, OUTPUT out_txt_SampleId, OUTPUT out_dtb_DataCollection
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO $'''%''%'''
    SET out_txt_SampleId TO $'''%''%'''
    SET out_dtb_DataCollection TO {{ }}
    IF (File.IfFile.DoesNotExist File: in_txt_InputFilePath) THEN
        SET txt_ErrorMessage TO 'File not found at: ' + in_txt_InputFilePath
        FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''Business Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ErrorMessage
    END
    SET num_WorksheetPosition TO 1
    BLOCK 'Read Excel block'
ON BLOCK ERROR
    CALL 'Close Application' In_txt_AppProcessName: GLOBAL.txt_ExcelProcessName
    CALL 'Get Error'
    THROW ERROR
END
        Excel.LaunchExcel.LaunchAndOpenUnderExistingProcess Path: in_txt_InputFilePath Visible: False ReadOnly: False UseMachineLocale: False Instance=> ins_ExcelInstance
        Excel.GetAllWorksheets Instance: ins_ExcelInstance Worksheets=> lst_SheetNames
        Excel.SetActiveWorksheet.ActivateWorksheetByIndex Instance: ins_ExcelInstance Index: num_WorksheetPosition
        Excel.ReadFromExcel.ReadAllCells Instance: ins_ExcelInstance GetCellContentsMode: Excel.GetCellContentsMode.PlainText FirstLineIsHeader: True RangeValue=> dtb_ExcelData
        Excel.CloseExcel.Close Instance: ins_ExcelInstance
    END
    IF dtb_ExcelData.IsEmpty = True THEN
        SET txt_ErrorMessage TO 'Data not available at: ' + lst_SheetNames[num_WorksheetPosition - 1]
        FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''Business Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ErrorMessage
    END
    SET txt_SampleId TO $'''%''%'''
    Scripting.RunDotNetScript Imports: $'''System.Data''' Language: System.DotNetActionLanguageType.CSharp Script: $'''string sampleId = \"\";
int headerRowIndex = -1;
int baseCol = -1;

//--------------------------------------------------
// Find Sample ID row and Header row
//--------------------------------------------------
for (int r = 0; r < ExcelData.Rows.Count; r++)
{
    for (int c = 0; c < ExcelData.Columns.Count; c++)
    {
        string value = Convert.ToString(ExcelData.Rows[r][c]).Trim();

        if (value.Equals(\"Sample ID\", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            if (c + 1 < ExcelData.Columns.Count)
            {
                sampleId =
                    Convert.ToString(ExcelData.Rows[r][c + 1]).Trim();
            }
        }

        if (value.Equals(\"Matrix Reference\", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            headerRowIndex = r;
            baseCol = c;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (headerRowIndex >= 0)
        break;
}

if (headerRowIndex < 0)
    throw new Exception(\"Header row not found.\");

if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sampleId))
    throw new Exception(\"Sample ID not found.\");

//--------------------------------------------------
// Create output table
//--------------------------------------------------
DataTable finalTable = new DataTable();

finalTable.Columns.Add(\"Matrix Reference\");
finalTable.Columns.Add(\"Method Code\");
finalTable.Columns.Add(\"SGS Analysis Name\");
finalTable.Columns.Add(\"Identity\");
finalTable.Columns.Add(\"Component\");
finalTable.Columns.Add(\"Results\");
finalTable.Columns.Add(\"Units\");

//--------------------------------------------------
// Fill-down variables
//--------------------------------------------------
string matrixReference = \"\";
string methodCode = \"\";
string analysisName = \"\";
string identity = \"\";

//--------------------------------------------------
// Transform
//--------------------------------------------------
for (int r = headerRowIndex + 1; r < ExcelData.Rows.Count; r++)
{
    DataRow row = ExcelData.Rows[r];

    string currentMatrix =
        Convert.ToString(row[baseCol + 0]).Trim();

    string currentMethod =
        Convert.ToString(row[baseCol + 1]).Trim();

    string currentAnalysis =
        Convert.ToString(row[baseCol + 2]).Trim();

    string currentIdentity =
        Convert.ToString(row[baseCol + 3]).Trim();

    string component =
        Convert.ToString(row[baseCol + 4]).Trim();

    string result =
        Convert.ToString(row[baseCol + 5]).Trim();

    string units =
        Convert.ToString(row[baseCol + 6]).Trim();

    // Skip non-data rows
    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(component))
        continue;

    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(currentMatrix))
        matrixReference = currentMatrix;

    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(currentMethod))
        methodCode = currentMethod;

    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(currentAnalysis))
        analysisName = currentAnalysis;

    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(currentIdentity))
        identity = currentIdentity;

    finalTable.Rows.Add(
        matrixReference,
        methodCode,
        analysisName,
        identity,
        component,
        result,
        units
    );
}

//--------------------------------------------------
// Return outputs
//--------------------------------------------------
SampleId = sampleId;
FinalTable = finalTable;''' @'name:ExcelData': dtb_ExcelData @'type:ExcelData': $'''Datatable''' @'direction:ExcelData': $'''In''' @'name:SampleId': $'''''' @'type:SampleId': $'''String''' @'direction:SampleId': $'''Out''' @'name:FinalTable': $'''''' @'type:FinalTable': $'''Datatable''' @'direction:FinalTable': $'''Out''' @SampleId=> txt_SampleId @FinalTable=> dtb_FinalTable
    ON ERROR
        CALL 'Get Error'
        THROW ERROR
    END
    SET dtb_ExcelData TO dtb_FinalTable
    DISABLE GOTO 'End File Process'
    LABEL 'End File Process'
    SET out_dtb_DataCollection TO dtb_ExcelData
    SET out_txt_SampleId TO txt_SampleId
END FUNCTION
==================================>

FUNCTION 'Launch Application' GLOBAL
    IF (System.IfProcess.ProcessIsRunning ProcessName: txt_AppProcessName) THEN
        WAIT (UIAutomation.WaitForWindowContent.WindowToContainElement Element: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Title Bar']) FOR num_Delay_M 
        ON ERROR
            SET flg_IsAppRunning TO False
            SET flg_IsLoggedIn TO False
            GOTO 'Launch application'
        END
        SET flg_IsAppRunning TO True
        EXIT FUNCTION
    END
    LABEL 'Launch application'
    IF flg_IsAppRunning = False THEN
        CALL 'Close Application' In_txt_AppProcessName: txt_AppProcessName
        LOOP num_AppRetryCount FROM 0 TO num_MaxRetryLimit - 1 STEP 1
            BLOCK 'App Start block'
ON BLOCK ERROR all
    SET flg_Screenshot TO True
    CALL 'Get Error'
    CALL 'Close Application' In_txt_AppProcessName: txt_AppProcessName
END
                System.RunApplication.RunApplicationAndWaitToLoad ApplicationPath: obj_Config['App_SampleManagerExePath'] WindowStyle: System.ProcessWindowStyle.Normal Timeout: num_App_WaitTime ProcessId=> txt_AppProcessId
                WAIT (UIAutomation.WaitForWindow.ToOpen Window: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS Login\'']['Title Bar'] FocusWindow: True) FOR num_Delay_L
                SET flg_IsAppRunning TO True
                EXIT LOOP
            END
            WAIT num_Delay_S * num_AppRetryCount
        END
        IF (flg_IsAppRunning = False AND num_AppRetryCount >= num_MaxRetryLimit) = True THEN
            FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''System Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: $'''Failed to start process \'%txt_AppProcessName%\''''
        END
        IF flg_IsLoggedIn = False THEN
            CALL 'Log In Application' In_txt_AppUsername: txt_AppUsername In_txt_AppPassword: txt_AppPassword Out_flg_Success=> flg_IsLoggedIn
            IF flg_IsLoggedIn = False THEN
                FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''System Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ExceptionType + ': ' + txt_ExceptionMessage
            END
        END
    END
END FUNCTION

FUNCTION 'Get Results by Analysis and SampleId' in_txt_Analysis, in_txt_SampleId
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO $'''%''%'''
    SET num_RetryCount TO 0
    LOOP num_RetryCount FROM 0 TO GLOBAL.num_MaxRetryLimit - 1 STEP 1
        IF (UIAutomation.IfWindow.IsOpen Window: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Window \'Result Entry\''] InstantCheck: False) THEN
            BLOCK 'Results entry block'
ON BLOCK ERROR
    SET GLOBAL.flg_Screenshot TO True
    CALL 'Get Error'
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO GLOBAL.txt_ExceptionMessage
END
                UIAutomation.FocusWindow.Focus Window: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Window \'Result Entry\'']
                IF (UIAutomation.IfWindowContains.WindowContainsElement Element: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Title \'Result Entry\''] InstantCheck: False) THEN
                    UIAutomation.FocusTextField TextField: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Edit \'Analysis\'']
                    UIAutomation.GetElementDetails Element: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Edit \'Analysis\''] AttributeName: $'''Own Text''' BringToFront: True AttributeValue=> txt_AnalysisValue
                    UIAutomation.GetElementDetails Element: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Edit \'Sample id\''] AttributeName: $'''Own Text''' BringToFront: True AttributeValue=> txt_SampleValue
                    IF txt_AnalysisValue.ToLower.Trimmed <> in_txt_Analysis.ToLower.Trimmed THEN
                        UIAutomation.PopulateTextField.PopulateTextField TextField: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Edit \'Analysis\''] Text: in_txt_Analysis Mode: UIAutomation.PopulateTextMode.Replace ClickType: UIAutomation.PopulateMouseClickType.SingleClick
                    END
                    UIAutomation.FocusTextField TextField: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Edit \'Sample id\'']
                    IF txt_SampleValue.Trimmed <> in_txt_SampleId.Trimmed THEN
                        UIAutomation.PopulateTextField.PopulateTextField TextField: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Edit \'Sample id\''] Text: in_txt_SampleId Mode: UIAutomation.PopulateTextMode.Replace ClickType: UIAutomation.PopulateMouseClickType.SingleClick
                    END
                    UIAutomation.PressButton Button: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Button \'Results...\''] Simulate: False
                    WAIT (UIAutomation.WaitForWindowContent.WindowToContainElement Element: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Window \'Result Entry Analysis\'']) FOR GLOBAL.num_Delay_M 
                    ON ERROR

                    END
                    IF (UIAutomation.IfWindowContains.WindowContainsElement Element: appmask['Window \'MESSAGE\''] InstantCheck: False) THEN
                        UIAutomation.GetElementDetails Element: appmask['Window \'MESSAGE\'']['Text \'No tests have been assigned to this sample\''] AttributeName: $'''Own Text''' BringToFront: True AttributeValue=> txt_ErrorMessage
                        UIAutomation.Click.Click Element: appmask['Window \'MESSAGE\'']['Button \'OK\''] ClickType: UIAutomation.ClickType.LeftClick MousePositionRelativeToElement: UIAutomation.RectangleEdgePoint.MiddleCenter OffsetX: 0 OffsetY: 0
                        FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''Business Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ErrorMessage
                    END
                END
                EXIT FUNCTION
            END
        ELSE
            CALL 'Open \'Entry By Test\''
        END
        WAIT GLOBAL.num_Delay_S
    END
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO txt_ErrorMessage + '. Results Entry window not found after ' + GLOBAL.num_MaxRetryLimit + ' retry attempts'
    LABEL 'Error block'
    FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''System Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ErrorMessage
END FUNCTION

FUNCTION 'Open \'Entry By Test\''
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO $'''%''%'''
    CALL 'Close Results Entry Analysis'
    CALL 'Close Results Entry'
    SET num_RetryCount TO 0
    LOOP num_RetryCount FROM 0 TO GLOBAL.num_MaxRetryLimit - 1 STEP 1
        BLOCK 'Explorer block'
ON BLOCK ERROR
    SET GLOBAL.flg_Screenshot TO True
    CALL 'Get Error'
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO GLOBAL.txt_ExceptionMessage
END
            IF (UIAutomation.IfWindow.IsOpen Window: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\''] InstantCheck: False) THEN
                UIAutomation.SetWindowState.SetState Window: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\''] State: UIAutomation.WindowState.Maximized

                    WAIT (UIAutomation.WaitForWindowContent.WindowToContainElement Element: appmask['Pane \'Results Menu\'']['MenuItem: Entry'])
                    UIAutomation.Click.Click Element: appmask['Pane \'Results Menu\'']['MenuItem: Entry'] ClickType: UIAutomation.ClickType.LeftClick MousePositionRelativeToElement: UIAutomation.RectangleEdgePoint.MiddleCenter OffsetX: 0 OffsetY: 0
                    WAIT (UIAutomation.WaitForWindowContent.WindowToContainElement Element: appmask['Pane \'Results SubMenu\''])

                    WAIT (UIAutomation.WaitForWindowContent.WindowToContainElement Element: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Window \'Result Entry\'']) FOR GLOBAL.num_Delay_S

    END

END FUNCTION

FUNCTION 'Get Components List' OUTPUT out_lst_ComponentNames
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO $'''%''%'''
    SET txt_ClipboardText TO $'''%''%'''
    IF (UIAutomation.IfWindow.IsOpen Window: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Window \'Result Entry Analysis\''] InstantCheck: False) THEN
        UIAutomation.SetWindowState.SetState Window: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Window \'Result Entry Analysis\''] State: UIAutomation.WindowState.Maximized
        IF (UIAutomation.IfWindowContains.WindowContainsElement Element: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Window \'Result Entry Analysis\''] InstantCheck: False) THEN
            SET num_RetryCount TO 0
            LOOP num_RetryCount FROM 0 TO GLOBAL.num_MaxRetryLimit - 1 STEP 1
                BLOCK 'Get Component block'
ON BLOCK ERROR
    SET GLOBAL.flg_Screenshot TO True
    CALL 'Get Error'
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO 'Failed to find Components on screen - ' + GLOBAL.txt_ExceptionMessage
    GOTO 'Error block'
END
                    Clipboard.Clear _
                    UIAutomation.FocusWindow.Focus Window: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Window \'Result Entry Analysis\'']
                    MouseAndKeyboard.SendKeys.FocusAndSendKeys TextToSend: $'''{Control}({A})''' DelayBetweenKeystrokes: 3 SendTextAsHardwareKeys: False
                    UIAutomation.FocusWindow.Focus Window: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Window \'Result Entry Analysis\'']
                    MouseAndKeyboard.SendKeys.FocusAndSendKeys TextToSend: $'''{Control}({C})''' DelayBetweenKeystrokes: 3 SendTextAsHardwareKeys: False
                    Clipboard.GetText Text=> txt_ClipboardText
                END
                IF txt_ClipboardText.Trimmed.Length > 0 THEN
                    EXIT LOOP
                END
            END
            Text.SplitText.Split Text: txt_ClipboardText StandardDelimiter: Text.StandardDelimiter.NewLine DelimiterTimes: 1 Result=> lst_TextList
            Variables.CreateNewDatatable InputTable: { ^['Analysis', 'Component Name', 'Type', 'Value', 'Units', 'Status'], [$'''''', $'''''', $'''''', $'''''', $'''''', $''''''] } DataTable=> dtb_DataTable
            LOOP FOREACH txt_CurrentItem IN lst_TextList
                IF txt_CurrentItem.Trimmed.Length > 0 THEN
                    Text.SplitText.Split Text: txt_CurrentItem StandardDelimiter: Text.StandardDelimiter.Tab DelimiterTimes: 1 Result=> lst_RowList
                    Variables.AddRowToDataTable.AppendRowToDataTable DataTable: dtb_DataTable RowToAdd: lst_RowList
                END
            END
            Variables.DeleteEmptyRowsFromDataTable DataTable: dtb_DataTable
            Variables.RetrieveDataTableColumnIntoList DataTable: dtb_DataTable ColumnNameOrIndex: $'''Component Name''' ColumnAsList=> lst_ComponentList
            SET out_lst_ComponentNames TO lst_ComponentList
            EXIT FUNCTION
        END
    END
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO txt_ErrorMessage + '. Results Entry Analysis window not found'
    LABEL 'Error block'
    FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''System Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ErrorMessage
END FUNCTION

FUNCTION 'Close Results Entry Analysis'
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO $'''%''%'''
    IF (UIAutomation.IfWindow.IsOpen Window: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Window \'Result Entry Analysis\''] InstantCheck: False) THEN
        UIAutomation.SetWindowState.SetState Window: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Window \'Result Entry Analysis\''] State: UIAutomation.WindowState.Maximized
        IF (UIAutomation.IfWindowContains.WindowContainsElement Element: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Window \'Result Entry Analysis\''] InstantCheck: False) THEN
            BLOCK 'Close Entry analysis block'
ON BLOCK ERROR all
    SET GLOBAL.flg_Screenshot TO True
    CALL 'Get Error'
END
                UIAutomation.PressButton Button: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Button \'Close\' 2'] Simulate: False
                WAIT (UIAutomation.WaitForWindowContent.WindowToContainElement Element: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Window \'Result Entry\'']) FOR GLOBAL.num_Delay_S
            END
            EXIT FUNCTION
        END
    END
END FUNCTION

FUNCTION 'Close Results Entry'
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO $'''%''%'''
    IF (UIAutomation.IfWindow.IsOpen Window: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Window \'Result Entry\''] InstantCheck: False) THEN
        IF (UIAutomation.IfWindowContains.WindowContainsElement Element: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Button \'Close\''] InstantCheck: False) THEN
            BLOCK 'Close Entry analysis block'
ON BLOCK ERROR all
    SET GLOBAL.flg_Screenshot TO True
    CALL 'Get Error'
END
                UIAutomation.PressButton Button: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Button \'Close\''] Simulate: False
                WAIT (UIAutomation.WaitForWindow.ToClose Window: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Window \'Result Entry\'']) FOR GLOBAL.num_Delay_S
            END
            EXIT FUNCTION
        END
    END
END FUNCTION

FUNCTION 'Set Results Entry' In_txt_ComponentName, In_lst_ComponentNamesList, In_txt_ComponentValue, OUTPUT Out_txt_EntryStatus, OUTPUT Out_txt_EntryError, OUTPUT Out_txt_Unauthorize, OUTPUT Out_txt_OutputValue

    SET Out_txt_OutputValue TO $'''%''%'''

            ON ERROR
                CALL 'Get Error'

                        GOTO Authorize

                        SET txt_ErrorMessage TO txt_ErrorMessage + 'Unable to read value in Result Entry Analysis Screen' + GLOBAL.LastError.Message
                        THROW ERROR

                    ON ERROR

                    GOTO 'End'
                ELSE

            SET Out_txt_EntryError TO $'''%'Parameter Not Matched'%'''
            LABEL 'End'
            SET Out_txt_OutputValue TO txt_AttributeValue
            EXIT FUNCTION

FUNCTION 'Enter Results in App' In_dtb_FinalProduct, txt_SampleId, OUTPUT out_dtb_filteredTable
    **REGION Initialise Variables
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO $'''%''%'''
    SET num_RowIndex TO 0
    SET num_RunCount TO 0
    SET num_RetryCount TO 0
    Variables.AddColumnToDataTable.AppendColumnToDataTable DataTable: In_dtb_FinalProduct ColumnName: $'''OutputValue'''
    Variables.AddColumnToDataTable.AppendColumnToDataTable DataTable: In_dtb_FinalProduct ColumnName: $'''Unauthorize'''
    Variables.AddColumnToDataTable.AppendColumnToDataTable DataTable: In_dtb_FinalProduct ColumnName: $'''EntryStatus'''
    Variables.AddColumnToDataTable.AppendColumnToDataTable DataTable: In_dtb_FinalProduct ColumnName: $'''EntryError'''
    Variables.CreateNewList List=> lst_MissingIdList
    Variables.CreateNewDatatable InputTable: { } DataTable=> out_dtb_filteredTable
    **ENDREGION
    BLOCK 'Data entry block'
ON BLOCK ERROR
    CALL 'Get Error'
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO GLOBAL.LastError
    GOTO 'Error block'
END
        LOOP FOREACH dtr_CurrentItem IN In_dtb_FinalProduct
            SET txt_Unauthorize TO $'''%''%'''
            SET txt_OutputValue TO $'''%''%'''
            SET txt_EntryStatus TO $'''%''%'''
            SET txt_EntryError TO $'''%''%'''
            **REGION Data Validation - SampleId, Indentity, Results
            IF txt_SampleId = $'''%''%''' THEN
                SET txt_Unauthorize TO $'''%'NULL'%'''
                SET txt_OutputValue TO $'''%'NULL'%'''
                SET txt_EntryStatus TO $'''%'NULL'%'''
                SET txt_EntryError TO $'''%'The Sample ID value is empty'%'''
                GOTO 'Summary block'
            END
            IF dtr_CurrentItem['Results'] = $'''%''%''' THEN
                SET txt_Unauthorize TO $'''%'NULL'%'''
                SET txt_OutputValue TO $'''%'NULL'%'''
                SET txt_EntryStatus TO $'''%'NULL'%'''
                SET txt_EntryError TO $'''%'NULL'%'''
                GOTO 'Summary block'
            END
            IF dtr_CurrentItem['Identity'] = $'''%''%''' THEN
                SET txt_Unauthorize TO $'''%'NULL'%'''
                SET txt_OutputValue TO $'''%'NULL'%'''
                SET txt_EntryStatus TO $'''%'NULL'%'''
                SET txt_EntryError TO $'''%'The Identity value not found in excel file'%'''
                GOTO 'Summary block'
            END
            Variables.FilterList ListToFilter: lst_MissingIdList FilterParameters: { ^['RULE', 'OPERATOR', 'VALUE'], [$'''''', $'''Equal''', dtr_CurrentItem['Identity']] } IgnoreCase: False FilteredList=> lst_FilteredList
            IF lst_FilteredList.Count > 0 THEN
                NEXT LOOP
            END
            **ENDREGION
            SET num_RunCount TO num_RunCount + 1
            IF num_RunCount = 1 THEN
                SET txt_Prev_Identity TO dtr_CurrentItem['Identity']
                CALL 'Open \'Entry By Test\''
                GOTO 'Navigate & Fetch Component List'
            ELSE
                IF dtr_CurrentItem['Identity'] = txt_Prev_Identity THEN
                    SET txt_Prev_Identity TO dtr_CurrentItem['Identity']
                    IF txt_EntryError = GLOBAL.obj_Config['Err_ProductException'] THEN
                        SET num_RowIndex TO num_RowIndex + 1
                        NEXT LOOP
                    ELSE
                        GOTO 'Data Entry to SampleManager'
                    END
                ELSE
                    DISABLE GOTO 'Summary block'
                    CALL 'Close Results Entry Analysis'
                    CALL 'Open \'Entry By Test\''
                END
            END
            **REGION Navigate to Results Analysis & Fetch component Name
            LABEL 'Navigate & Fetch Component List'
            SET flg_Success TO False
            BLOCK 'Fetch Sample ID block'
ON BLOCK ERROR
    CALL 'Get Error'
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO txt_ErrorMessage + ': ' + GLOBAL.LastError.Message
END
                CALL 'Get Results by Analysis and SampleId' in_txt_Analysis: dtr_CurrentItem['Identity'] in_txt_SampleId: txt_SampleId
                SET flg_Success TO True
            END
            IF flg_Success = False THEN
                Variables.AddItemToList Item: dtr_CurrentItem['Identity'] List: lst_MissingIdList
                SET txt_Unauthorize TO $'''%'UnAuthorise'%'''
                SET txt_OutputValue TO $'''%'NULL'%'''
                SET txt_EntryStatus TO $'''%'FAIL'%'''
                SET txt_EntryError TO GLOBAL.obj_Config['Err_ProductException']
                GOTO 'Summary block'
            END
            LOOP num_RetryCount FROM 1 TO GLOBAL.num_MaxRetryLimit STEP 1
                BLOCK 'Component names block'
ON BLOCK ERROR
    CALL 'Get Error'
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO txt_ErrorMessage + ': ' + GLOBAL.LastError.Message
END
                    CALL 'Get Components List' out_lst_ComponentNames=> lst_ComponentNames
                    IF lst_ComponentNames.Count > 0 THEN
                        LOOP num_LoopIndex FROM 0 TO lst_ComponentNames.Count - 1 STEP 1
                            Text.ChangeCase Text: lst_ComponentNames[num_LoopIndex] NewCase: Text.CaseOption.LowerCase Result=> txt_CurrentComponent
                            SET lst_ComponentNames[num_LoopIndex] TO txt_CurrentComponent
                        END
                        EXIT LOOP
                    END
                END
            END
            IF lst_ComponentNames.Count = 0 THEN
                GOTO 'Error block'
            END
            **ENDREGION
            SET num_RetryCount TO 0
            **REGION Data Entry to SampleManager
            LABEL 'Data Entry to SampleManager'
            IF dtr_CurrentItem['Component'] = $'''%''%''' THEN
                SET txt_Unauthorize TO $'''%'NULL'%'''
                SET txt_OutputValue TO $'''%'NULL'%'''
                SET txt_EntryStatus TO $'''%'FAIL'%'''
                SET txt_EntryError TO $'''%'The Component value not found in excel file'%'''
                GOTO 'Summary block'
            END
            IF dtr_CurrentItem['Results'] = $'''%''%''' THEN
                SET txt_Unauthorize TO $'''%'UnAuthorise'%'''
                SET txt_OutputValue TO $'''%'NULL'%'''
                SET txt_EntryStatus TO $'''%'FAIL'%'''
                SET txt_EntryError TO $'''%'The Identity value not found in excel file'%'''
                GOTO 'Summary block'
            END
            SET flg_Success TO False
            LOOP num_RetryCount FROM 1 TO GLOBAL.num_MaxRetryLimit STEP 1
                BLOCK 'Component data entry block'
ON BLOCK ERROR
    CALL 'Get Error'
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO txt_ErrorMessage + ': ' + GLOBAL.LastError.Message
END
                    CALL 'Set Results Entry' In_txt_ComponentName: dtr_CurrentItem['Component'] In_lst_ComponentNamesList: lst_ComponentNames In_txt_ComponentValue: dtr_CurrentItem['Results'] Out_txt_EntryStatus=> txt_EntryStatus Out_txt_EntryError=> txt_EntryError Out_txt_Unauthorize=> txt_Unauthorize Out_txt_OutputValue=> txt_OutputValue
                    SET flg_Success TO True
                    EXIT LOOP
                END
            END
            IF flg_Success = False THEN
                GOTO 'Error block'
            END
            IF txt_EntryError = $'''Parameter Not Matched''' THEN
                SET txt_EntryError TO 'Component (' + dtr_CurrentItem['Component'] + ') not found in Sample Manager'
                SET txt_Unauthorize TO $'''%'UnAuthorise'%'''
                SET txt_OutputValue TO $'''%'NULL'%'''
                SET txt_EntryStatus TO $'''%'FAIL'%'''
                GOTO 'Summary block'
            END
            IF txt_OutputValue = $'''%''%''' THEN
                SET txt_OutputValue TO $'''%'NULL'%'''
            END
            **ENDREGION
            LABEL 'Summary block'
            Variables.ModifyDataTableItem DataTable: In_dtb_FinalProduct ColumnNameOrIndex: $'''Unauthorize''' RowIndex: num_RowIndex Value: txt_Unauthorize
            Variables.ModifyDataTableItem DataTable: In_dtb_FinalProduct ColumnNameOrIndex: $'''OutputValue''' RowIndex: num_RowIndex Value: txt_OutputValue
            Variables.ModifyDataTableItem DataTable: In_dtb_FinalProduct ColumnNameOrIndex: $'''EntryStatus''' RowIndex: num_RowIndex Value: txt_EntryStatus
            Variables.ModifyDataTableItem DataTable: In_dtb_FinalProduct ColumnNameOrIndex: $'''EntryError''' RowIndex: num_RowIndex Value: txt_EntryError
            SET num_RowIndex TO num_RowIndex + 1
        END
    END
    CALL 'Close Results Entry Analysis'
    CALL 'Close Results Entry'
    SET out_dtb_filteredTable TO In_dtb_FinalProduct
    EXIT FUNCTION
    LABEL 'Error block'
    FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''System Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ErrorMessage
END FUNCTION

==================================>

FUNCTION 'Create Summary Report' SummaryData, In_txt_DestinationPath, OUTPUT out_txt_DestinationPath
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO $'''%''%'''
    IF SummaryData.IsEmpty = True THEN
        GOTO 'End File Process'
    END
    IF (File.IfFile.DoesNotExist File: GLOBAL.txt_SummaryFilePath) THEN
        SET txt_ErrorMessage TO 'File not found at: ' + GLOBAL.txt_SummaryFilePath
        FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''Business Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ErrorMessage
    END
    DateTime.GetCurrentDateTime.Local DateTimeFormat: DateTime.DateTimeFormat.DateAndTime CurrentDateTime=> dt_CurrentDateTime
    Text.ConvertDateTimeToText.FromCustomDateTime DateTime: dt_CurrentDateTime CustomFormat: $'''yyyyMMdd''' Result=> txt_FormattedDateTime
    Text.Replace.ReplaceText Text: txt_FormattedDateTime TextToFind: $''':''' IgnoreCase: True ReplaceWith: $'''-''' ActivateEscapeSequences: False ComparisonType: Text.TextComparisonType.CultureSensitive Result=> txt_FormattedDateTime
    Text.Replace.ReplaceText Text: txt_FormattedDateTime TextToFind: $'''T''' IgnoreCase: True ReplaceWith: $'''-''' ActivateEscapeSequences: False ComparisonType: Text.TextComparisonType.CultureSensitive Result=> txt_FormattedDateTime
    Text.Replace.ReplaceText Text: GLOBAL.obj_Config['Path_SummarySaveAs'] TextToFind: $'''<<date>>''' IgnoreCase: True ReplaceWith: txt_FormattedDateTime ActivateEscapeSequences: False ComparisonType: Text.TextComparisonType.CultureSensitive Result=> txt_SummaryReportName
    SET txt_DestinationPath TO $'''%In_txt_DestinationPath%\\%txt_SummaryReportName%'''
    SET num_WorksheetPosition TO 1
    BLOCK 'Write Summary block'
ON BLOCK ERROR
    CALL 'Close Application' In_txt_AppProcessName: GLOBAL.txt_ExcelProcessName
    SET GLOBAL.flg_Screenshot TO False
    CALL 'Get Error'
    SET txt_ErrorMessage TO $'''Unable to write the collection data into Summary Workbook'''
    GOTO 'Error File Process'
END
        Excel.LaunchExcel.LaunchAndOpenUnderExistingProcess Path: GLOBAL.txt_SummaryFilePath Visible: False ReadOnly: False UseMachineLocale: False Instance=> ins_ExcelInstance
        Excel.SetActiveWorksheet.ActivateWorksheetByIndex Instance: ins_ExcelInstance Index: num_WorksheetPosition
        Excel.WriteToExcel.WriteCell Instance: ins_ExcelInstance Value: SummaryData Column: 1 Row: 2
        Excel.SaveExcel.SaveAs Instance: ins_ExcelInstance DocumentFormat: Excel.ExcelFormat.FromExtension DocumentPath: txt_DestinationPath
        Excel.CloseExcel.Close Instance: ins_ExcelInstance
    END
    LABEL 'End File Process'
    SET out_txt_DestinationPath TO txt_DestinationPath
    EXIT FUNCTION
    LABEL 'Error File Process'
    FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''System Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ErrorMessage
END FUNCTION

FUNCTION 'Send Email' In_txt_To_Email, In_txt_Cc_Email, In_txt_Subject, In_lst_Attachments
    DISABLE SET txt_ErrorMessage TO $'''%''%'''
    DateTime.GetCurrentDateTime.Local DateTimeFormat: DateTime.DateTimeFormat.DateAndTime CurrentDateTime=> dt_CurrentDateTime
    Text.ConvertDateTimeToText.FromDateTime DateTime: dt_CurrentDateTime StandardFormat: Text.WellKnownDateTimeFormat.LongDate Result=> txt_FormattedDateTime
    SET txt_MailBody TO GLOBAL.obj_Config['Mail_EmailBodyReport']
    Text.Replace.ReplaceText Text: txt_MailBody TextToFind: $'''{{RunDate}}''' IgnoreCase: False ReplaceWith: txt_FormattedDateTime ActivateEscapeSequences: False ComparisonType: Text.TextComparisonType.CultureSensitive Result=> txt_MailBody
    Text.Replace.ReplaceText Text: txt_MailBody TextToFind: $'''{{ResourceName}}''' IgnoreCase: False ReplaceWith: GLOBAL.txt_ResourceName ActivateEscapeSequences: False ComparisonType: Text.TextComparisonType.CultureSensitive Result=> txt_MailBody
    Text.Replace.ReplaceText Text: txt_MailBody TextToFind: $'''{{Username}}''' IgnoreCase: False ReplaceWith: GLOBAL.txt_UserName ActivateEscapeSequences: False ComparisonType: Text.TextComparisonType.CultureSensitive Result=> txt_MailBody
    Text.Replace.ReplaceText Text: txt_MailBody TextToFind: $'''{{ProcessName}}''' IgnoreCase: False ReplaceWith: GLOBAL.txt_ProcessName ActivateEscapeSequences: False ComparisonType: Text.TextComparisonType.CultureSensitive Result=> txt_MailBody
    IF (In_txt_To_Email + In_txt_Cc_Email) = $'''%''%''' THEN
        FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''System Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: $'''Recipients cannot be empty or blank'''
    END
    IF In_txt_Subject = $'''%''%''' THEN
        FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''System Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: $'''Subject cannot be empty or blank'''
    END
    Variables.CreateNewList List=> lst_AttachmentsList
    SET lst_AttachmentsList TO []
    IF In_lst_Attachments.Count > 0 THEN
        LOOP FOREACH CurrentItem IN In_lst_Attachments
            File.GetPathPart File: CurrentItem RootPath=> txt_RootPath Directory=> txt_Directory FileName=> txt_FileName FileNameWithoutExtension=> FileNameNoExtension Extension=> txt_FileExtension
            File.ConvertToBase64 File: CurrentItem Base64Text=> txt_Base64Text
            SET obj_AttachmentObject TO { 'Name': txt_FileName, 'ContentBytes': txt_Base64Text }
            Variables.AddItemToList Item: obj_AttachmentObject List: lst_AttachmentsList
        END
    END
    @@'emailMessage/Sensitivity': ''

    DISABLE EXIT FUNCTION

FUNCTION 'Close Application' In_txt_AppProcessName
    IF In_txt_AppProcessName.Length > 0 THEN
        Text.SplitText.SplitWithDelimiter Text: In_txt_AppProcessName CustomDelimiter: $''',''' IsRegEx: False Result=> lst_AppProcessList
    END
    LOOP FOREACH txt_AppProcessName IN lst_AppProcessList
        BLOCK 'Close application block'
ON BLOCK ERROR

END
            IF (System.IfProcess.ProcessIsRunning ProcessName: txt_AppProcessName) THEN
                System.TerminateProcess.TerminateProcessByName ProcessName: txt_AppProcessName
                WAIT (System.WaitForProcess.ProcessToStop ProcessName: txt_AppProcessName) FOR GLOBAL.num_Delay_XL
            END
            NEXT LOOP
        END
        LABEL 'Force End'
        Scripting.RunPowershellScript.RunScript Script: $'''Stop-Process -Name \"%txt_AppProcessName%\" -Force''' ScriptOutput=> txt_PowershellOutput
        ON ERROR

        END
    END
END FUNCTION

==================================>

FUNCTION 'Create New Folder' In_txt_RootFolder, In_txt_FolderName, OUTPUT Out_txt_NewFolder
    Text.Replace.ReplaceText Text: In_txt_FolderName TextToFind: $'''\\''' IgnoreCase: False ReplaceWith: $'''%''%''' ActivateEscapeSequences: False ComparisonType: Text.TextComparisonType.CultureSensitive Result=> In_txt_FolderName
    SET txt_NewFolder TO In_txt_RootFolder + '\\' + In_txt_FolderName
    IF (Folder.IfFolderExists.DoesNotExist Path: txt_NewFolder) THEN
        Folder.Create FolderPath: In_txt_RootFolder FolderName: In_txt_FolderName Folder=> txt_NewFolder
        ON ERROR

        END
    END
    SET Out_txt_NewFolder TO txt_NewFolder
END FUNCTION

==================================>

FUNCTION 'Log In Application' In_txt_AppUsername, In_txt_AppPassword, OUTPUT Out_flg_Success
    SET flg_IsLoggedIn TO False
    SET num_RetryCount TO 0
    LOOP num_RetryCount FROM 0 TO GLOBAL.num_MaxRetryLimit - 1 STEP 1
        BLOCK 'Login block'
ON BLOCK ERROR all
    SET flg_Screenshot TO True
    CALL 'Get Error'
END
            IF (UIAutomation.IfWindow.IsOpen Window: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS Login\''] InstantCheck: False) THEN
                WAIT (UIAutomation.WaitForWindowContent.WindowToContainElement Element: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS Login\'']['Label \'Username\'']) FOR GLOBAL.num_Delay_M
                UIAutomation.GetElementDetails Element: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS Login\'']['Edit \'Server\''] AttributeName: $'''Own Text''' BringToFront: True AttributeValue=> txt_AppServerName
                IF txt_AppServerName = $'''%''%''' THEN
                    FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''System Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: $'''Server field is not configured in the application'''
                END
                UIAutomation.PopulateTextField.PopulateTextField TextField: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS Login\'']['Edit \'Username\''] Text: In_txt_AppUsername Mode: UIAutomation.PopulateTextMode.Replace ClickType: UIAutomation.PopulateMouseClickType.SingleClick
                UIAutomation.PopulateTextField.PopulateTextField TextField: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS Login\'']['Edit \'Password\''] Text: In_txt_AppPassword Mode: UIAutomation.PopulateTextMode.Replace ClickType: UIAutomation.PopulateMouseClickType.SingleClick
                UIAutomation.Click.Click Element: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS Login\'']['Button \'Login\''] ClickType: UIAutomation.ClickType.LeftClick MousePositionRelativeToElement: UIAutomation.RectangleEdgePoint.MiddleCenter OffsetX: 0 OffsetY: 0
                WAIT (UIAutomation.WaitForWindowContent.WindowToContainElement Element: appmask['Window \'SampleManager LIMS - Explorer\'']['Title Bar']) FOR GLOBAL.num_Delay_L
                SET flg_IsLoggedIn TO True
                EXIT LOOP
            END
        END
        WAIT GLOBAL.num_Delay_S * GLOBAL.num_AppRetryCount
    END
    SET Out_flg_Success TO flg_IsLoggedIn
END FUNCTION

FUNCTION 'Mark Complete' In_obj_WorkQueueItem
    SET obj_WorkQueueItem TO In_obj_WorkQueueItem
    DateTime.GetCurrentDateTime.Local DateTimeFormat: DateTime.DateTimeFormat.DateAndTime CurrentDateTime=> dt_CurrentDateTime
    Text.ConvertDateTimeToText.FromDateTime DateTime: dt_CurrentDateTime StandardFormat: Text.WellKnownDateTimeFormat.SortableDateTime Result=> txt_FormattedDateTime
    WorkQueues.UpdateProcessingNotes.WithProcessingNotes WorkQueueItem: obj_WorkQueueItem ProcessingNotes: 'Processing completed at ' + txt_FormattedDateTime
    WorkQueues.UpdateWorkQueueItem.UpdateWithProcessingNotes WorkQueueItem: obj_WorkQueueItem Status: WorkQueues.WorkQueueItemStatus.Processed
    DISABLE Variables.IncreaseVariable Value: GLOBAL.num_ItemsCompleted IncrementValue: 1
END FUNCTION

==================================>

FUNCTION 'Mark Exception' In_obj_WorkQueueItem
    SET obj_WorkQueueItem TO In_obj_WorkQueueItem
    DateTime.GetCurrentDateTime.Local DateTimeFormat: DateTime.DateTimeFormat.DateAndTime CurrentDateTime=> dt_CurrentDateTime
    Text.ConvertDateTimeToText.FromDateTime DateTime: dt_CurrentDateTime StandardFormat: Text.WellKnownDateTimeFormat.SortableDateTime Result=> txt_FormattedDateTime
    DISABLE Variables.IncreaseVariable Value: GLOBAL.num_ItemsException IncrementValue: 1
    IF GLOBAL.txt_ExceptionType.ToLower = $'''system exception''' THEN
        WorkQueues.UpdateWorkQueueItem.UpdateWithProcessingNotes WorkQueueItem: obj_WorkQueueItem Status: WorkQueues.WorkQueueItemStatus.GenericException ProcessingResult: GLOBAL.txt_ExceptionMessage
        IF GLOBAL.txt_PreviousExceptionMessage = GLOBAL.txt_ExceptionMessage THEN
            Variables.IncreaseVariable Value: GLOBAL.num_ConsecutiveExcCount IncrementValue: 1
            IF GLOBAL.num_ConsecutiveExcCount >= GLOBAL.num_ConsecutiveExcLimit THEN
                CALL 'Send Consecutive Exception Mail' In_txt_To_Email: GLOBAL.obj_Config['Mail_SystemExceptionTo'] In_txt_Cc_Email: GLOBAL.obj_Config['Mail_SystemExceptionCc'] In_txt_Subject: $'''%GLOBAL.txt_ProcessName% - Consecutive error encountered''' In_txt_ErrorMessage: GLOBAL.txt_ExceptionMessage In_lst_Attachments: []
                WorkQueues.UpdateWorkQueueItem.UpdateWithProcessingNotes WorkQueueItem: obj_WorkQueueItem Status: WorkQueues.WorkQueueItemStatus.ITException ProcessingResult: GLOBAL.num_ConsecutiveExcLimit + ' consecutive incidents of ' + GLOBAL.txt_ExceptionType + ': ' + GLOBAL.txt_ExceptionMessage
            END
        ELSE
            SET GLOBAL.txt_PreviousExceptionMessage TO GLOBAL.txt_ExceptionMessage
            SET GLOBAL.num_ConsecutiveExcCount TO 0
        END
    ELSE IF GLOBAL.txt_ExceptionType.ToLower = $'''system unavailable exception''' THEN
        WorkQueues.UpdateWorkQueueItem.UpdateWithProcessingNotes WorkQueueItem: obj_WorkQueueItem Status: WorkQueues.WorkQueueItemStatus.ITException ProcessingResult: GLOBAL.num_ConsecutiveExcLimit + ' consecutive incidents of ' + GLOBAL.txt_ExceptionType + ': ' + GLOBAL.txt_ExceptionMessage
    ELSE
        WorkQueues.UpdateWorkQueueItem.UpdateWithProcessingNotes WorkQueueItem: obj_WorkQueueItem Status: WorkQueues.WorkQueueItemStatus.BusinessException ProcessingResult: GLOBAL.txt_ExceptionMessage
        CALL 'Send Business Exception Mail' In_txt_To_Email: GLOBAL.obj_Config['Mail_BusinessExceptionTo'] In_txt_Cc_Email: GLOBAL.obj_Config['Mail_BusinessExceptionCc'] In_txt_Subject: $'''%GLOBAL.txt_ProcessName% - Business Error''' In_txt_ErrorMessage: GLOBAL.txt_ExceptionMessage In_lst_Attachments: []
        SET GLOBAL.txt_PreviousExceptionMessage TO $'''%''%'''
        SET GLOBAL.num_ConsecutiveExcCount TO 0
    END
END FUNCTION

==================================>

FUNCTION 'Delete files from Folder' In_txt_FolderName
    SET txt_FolderName TO In_txt_FolderName
    IF txt_FolderName <> $'''%''%''' THEN
        IF NotEndsWith(txt_FolderName, $'''\\''', False) THEN
            SET txt_FolderName TO txt_FolderName + '\\'
        END
        IF (Folder.IfFolderExists.Exists Path: txt_FolderName) THEN
            Folder.Delete Folder: txt_FolderName
            ON ERROR

            END
        END
    END
END FUNCTION

==================================>

FUNCTION 'Move Emails' In_txt_MessageId, In_txt_DestinationFolder
    IF (In_txt_DestinationFolder <> '' AND In_txt_MessageId <> '') = True THEN
        @@connectionDisplayName: 'Office 365 Outlook'
External.InvokeCloudConnector Connection: '767fb7db-cc2e-48de-b921-e838c647a4e1' ConnectorId: '/providers/Microsoft.PowerApps/apis/shared_office365' OperationId: 'MarkAsRead_V3' @messageId: In_txt_MessageId @mailboxAddress: GLOBAL.txt_MailboxName @'body/isRead': $'''True'''
        @@folderPath: '%In_txt_DestinationFolder%'
@@connectionDisplayName: 'Office 365 Outlook'
External.InvokeCloudConnector Connection: '767fb7db-cc2e-48de-b921-e838c647a4e1' ConnectorId: '/providers/Microsoft.PowerApps/apis/shared_office365' OperationId: 'MoveV2' @messageId: In_txt_MessageId @folderPath: In_txt_DestinationFolder @mailboxAddress: GLOBAL.txt_MailboxName @MoveV2Response=> obj_MoveMailResponse
    END
END FUNCTION

FUNCTION 'Process Work Queue Items' GLOBAL
    Variables.CreateNewList List=> lst_AttachmentsList
    @@'InputSummaryValue:WORKQUEUE': '%txt_WorkQueueId%'
LOOP WHILE (WorkQueues.ProcessWorkQueueItem.ProcessWorkQueueItem WorkQueue: txt_WorkQueueId WorkQueueItem=> obj_WorkQueueItem)
        **REGION Initialise work queue
        SET lst_AttachmentsList TO []
        DISABLE SET num_CompletedCount TO 0
        DISABLE SET num_ExceptionCount TO 0
        DISABLE SET txt_ExceptionType TO $'''%''%'''
        DISABLE SET txt_ExceptionMessage TO $'''%''%'''
        SET flg_ErrorOccurred TO False
        SET lst_MailAttachments TO []
        SET txt_ItemProecssingNotes TO $'''%''%'''
        SET txt_MailId TO $'''%''%'''
        SET txt_ItemStatus TO $'''%''%'''
        **ENDREGION
        BLOCK 'Process items block'
ON BLOCK ERROR 'Business Exception' IsUserDefinedErrorCode: True
    SET flg_Screenshot TO False
    CALL 'Get Error'
    SET txt_ExceptionType TO $'''Business Exception'''
    SET flg_ErrorOccurred TO True
ON BLOCK ERROR
    SET flg_Screenshot TO True
    CALL 'Get Error'
    SET flg_ErrorOccurred TO True
END
            DateTime.GetCurrentDateTime.Local DateTimeFormat: DateTime.DateTimeFormat.DateAndTime CurrentDateTime=> dt_CurrentDateTime
            Text.ConvertDateTimeToText.FromDateTime DateTime: dt_CurrentDateTime StandardFormat: Text.WellKnownDateTimeFormat.SortableDateTime Result=> txt_ItemStartDateTime
            SET txt_ItemProecssingNotes TO txt_ItemProecssingNotes + 'Processing started at ' + txt_ItemStartDateTime
            WorkQueues.UpdateProcessingNotes.WithProcessingNotes WorkQueueItem: obj_WorkQueueItem ProcessingNotes: 'Processing started at ' + txt_ItemStartDateTime
            SET txt_ItemStatus TO $'''In Progress'''
            SET txt_ItemName TO obj_WorkQueueItem.Name
            Cryptography.DecryptText.DecryptTextWithAES Encoding: Cryptography.EncryptionEncoding.Unicode TextToDecrypt: obj_WorkQueueItem.Value DecryptionKey: txt_EncDecSecret_Key Padding: Cryptography.PaddingMode.PKCS7 KeySize: Cryptography.AESKeySize.Bits256 DecryptedText=> txt_ItemData
            IF txt_ItemData.Length = 0 THEN
                SET txt_ItemStatus TO $'''Failed'''
                SET txt_ExceptionMessage TO 'Item ' + txt_ItemName + ' has no value.'
                FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''Business Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ExceptionMessage
            END
            Variables.ConvertJsonToCustomObject Json: txt_ItemData CustomObject=> obj_QueueItemData
            SET txt_MailId TO obj_QueueItemData['Id']
            SET lst_MailAttachments TO obj_QueueItemData['MailAttachments']
            **REGION Mail attachment validation
            IF lst_MailAttachments.Count = 0 THEN
                SET txt_ItemStatus TO $'''Failed'''
                SET txt_ExceptionMessage TO 'Item ' + txt_ItemName + ' has no attachments.'
                FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''Business Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ExceptionMessage
            END
            IF lst_MailAttachments.Count > 1 THEN
                SET txt_ItemStatus TO $'''Failed'''
                SET txt_ExceptionMessage TO 'Item ' + txt_ItemName + ' has ' + lst_MailAttachments.Count + ' attachments.'
                FlowControl.ThrowCustomError CustomErrorCode: $'''Business Exception''' CustomErrorMessage: txt_ExceptionMessage
            END
            **ENDREGION
            **REGION Create Email folders
            CALL 'Create New Folder' In_txt_RootFolder: txt_EmailPath In_txt_FolderName: txt_ItemName Out_txt_NewFolder=> txt_EmailDownloadPath
            CALL 'Create New Folder' In_txt_RootFolder: txt_EmailDownloadPath In_txt_FolderName: obj_Config['Path_InputFile'] Out_txt_NewFolder=> txt_EmailInputsPath
            CALL 'Create New Folder' In_txt_RootFolder: txt_EmailDownloadPath In_txt_FolderName: obj_Config['Path_SummaryDestination'] Out_txt_NewFolder=> txt_EmailSummaryPath
            **ENDREGION
            LOOP FOREACH CurrentItem IN lst_MailAttachments
                SET txt_AttachmentName TO CurrentItem.Name
                SET txt_AttachmentFilepath TO CurrentItem.Filepath
                # Save attachment to input folder
                File.ConvertFromBase64 Base64Text: CurrentItem.ContentBytes File: txt_AttachmentFilepath IfFileExists: File.IfExists.Overwrite
                WorkQueues.UpdateProcessingNotes.WithProcessingNotes WorkQueueItem: obj_WorkQueueItem ProcessingNotes: $'''File downloaded and saved to Email inputs folder'''
                # Read data from the attachment - Extract Sample ID and LIMS data
                CALL 'Fetch Data from Excel file' in_txt_InputFilePath: txt_AttachmentFilepath out_txt_SampleId=> txt_SampleId out_dtb_DataCollection=> dtb_FileData
                WorkQueues.UpdateProcessingNotes.WithProcessingNotes WorkQueueItem: obj_WorkQueueItem ProcessingNotes: $'''Data fetched and formatted from the attachment'''
                # Enter all valid LIMS data into Sample Manager application
                CALL 'Enter Results in App' In_dtb_FinalProduct: dtb_FileData txt_SampleId: txt_SampleId out_dtb_filteredTable=> dtb_FinalData
                WorkQueues.UpdateProcessingNotes.WithProcessingNotes WorkQueueItem: obj_WorkQueueItem ProcessingNotes: $'''Data entry completed in application'''
                # Create summary report from the LIMS data and their entry status
                CALL 'Create Summary Report' SummaryData: dtb_FinalData In_txt_DestinationPath: txt_EmailSummaryPath out_txt_DestinationPath=> txt_DestinationPath
                WorkQueues.UpdateProcessingNotes.WithProcessingNotes WorkQueueItem: obj_WorkQueueItem ProcessingNotes: $'''Summary report created'''
                Variables.AddItemToList Item: txt_DestinationPath List: lst_AttachmentsList
                # Send report to business
                SET txt_ItemStatus TO $'''Completed'''
                CALL 'Send Email' In_txt_To_Email: obj_Config['Mail_BusinessTo'] In_txt_Cc_Email: obj_Config['Mail_BusinessCc'] In_txt_Subject: txt_ItemStatus + ': ' + obj_QueueItemData['Subject'] In_lst_Attachments: lst_AttachmentsList
                # Move emails & delete all files from local
                CALL 'Delete files from Folder' In_txt_FolderName: txt_EmailDownloadPath
                CALL 'Move Emails' In_txt_MessageId: txt_MailId In_txt_DestinationFolder: txt_MailCompletedFolder
            END
        END
        IF flg_ErrorOccurred = True THEN
            SET txt_ItemStatus TO $'''Failed'''
            GOTO 'Mark Item as Exception'
        END
        LABEL 'Mark Item as Completed'
        **REGION Completion
        CALL 'Mark Complete' In_obj_WorkQueueItem: obj_WorkQueueItem
        GOTO 'Reset All'
        **ENDREGION
        LABEL 'Mark Item as Exception'
        **REGION Exception
        CALL 'Mark Exception' In_obj_WorkQueueItem: obj_WorkQueueItem
        CALL 'Move Emails' In_txt_MessageId: txt_MailId In_txt_DestinationFolder: txt_MailExceptionFolder
        # # Close all applications
        CALL 'Close Application' In_txt_AppProcessName: txt_AppProcessName + ',' + txt_ExcelProcessName
        SET flg_IsAppRunning TO False
        SET flg_IsLoggedIn TO False
        **ENDREGION
        LABEL 'Reset All'
        **REGION Data Gateway
        SET txt_EventData TO $'''{
    \"StartDate\": \"%txt_ItemStartDateTime%\",
    \"CustomDataCollection\": {
        \"Rows\": [
            {
                \"ItemKey\": {
                    \"Value\": \"%obj_WorkQueueItem.Name%\"
                },
                \"ItemCreatedOn\": {
                    \"Value\": \"%obj_WorkQueueItem.ProcessingStartTime%\"
                },
                \"QueueItemStatus\": {
                    \"Value\": \"%obj_WorkQueueItem.StatusCode%\"
                },
                \"TotalWorkTimeInSeconds\": {
                    \"Value\": \"%obj_WorkQueueItem.ProcessingDuration%\"
                },
                \"ResourceVM\": {
                    \"Value\": \"%txt_ResourceName%\"
                },
                \"InputReceivedTime\": {
                    \"Value\": \"%obj_WorkQueueItem.ProcessingStartTime%\"
                },
                \"ItemId\": {
                    \"Value\": \"%obj_WorkQueueItem.Id%\"
                },
                \"ItemCompletedOn\": {
                    \"Value\": \"%obj_WorkQueueItem.CompletedOn%\"
                },
                \"FinalOutcome\": {
                    \"Value\": \"%txt_ItemStatus + ': ' + txt_ExceptionMessage%\"
                },
                \"ProcessName\": {
                    \"Value\": \"%txt_ProcessName%\"
                }
            }
        ]
    }
}'''
        @@flowname: 'DF_Utility_SQL_DataGateway'
External.RunFlow FlowId: '15ac1877-c8dc-4410-9947-98616833caaf' WaitToComplete: True @In_txt_Payload: txt_EventData @In_cred_DB_Password: txt_DbPassword @Out_txt_Payload=> Out_txt_Payload
        **ENDREGION
        SET txt_ExceptionType TO $'''%''%'''
        SET txt_ExceptionMessage TO $'''%''%'''
        SET lst_AttachmentsList TO []
        SET lst_MailAttachments TO []
        SET txt_ItemProecssingNotes TO $'''%''%'''
        SET txt_ItemStatus TO $'''%''%'''
        SET flg_ErrorOccurred TO False
        BLOCK 'Launch block'
ON BLOCK ERROR
    SET flg_Screenshot TO True
    CALL 'Get Error'
    THROW ERROR
END
            CALL 'Launch Application'
        END
    END
END FUNCTION
